  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Dipplin<br></strong><em>From Mask of Change</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Festival Lead<br>If Festival Grounds is in play, this Pokémon may use any attack it has twice. If the first attack Knocks Out your opponent's Active Pokémon, you may attack again after your opponent chooses a new Active Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[G] Do the Wave: 20x damage. This attack does 20 damage for each of your Benched Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Translator’s Note: </strong>“Festival Lead” does not work for attacks it can use but does not actually have. It can’t, for example, attack twice using an TM’s attack.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Goldeen<br></strong><em>From Mask of Change</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Festival Lead<br>If Festival Grounds is in play, this Pokémon may use any attack it has twice. If the first attack Knocks Out your opponent's Active Pokémon, you may attack again after your opponent chooses a new Active Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[C][C] Whirlpool: 10 damage. Flip a coin. If heads, discard an Energy from your opponent's Active Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Translator’s Note: </strong>“Festival Lead” does not work for attacks it can use but does not actually have. It can’t, for example, attack twice using an TM’s attack.</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Swirlix<br></strong><em>From Mask of Change</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Festival Lead<br>If Festival Grounds is in play, this Pokémon may use any attack it has twice. If the first attack Knocks Out your opponent's Active Pokémon, you may attack again after your opponent chooses a new Active Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[P] Sneaky Placement: Put 2 damage counters on 1 of your opponent's Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Translator’s Note: </strong>“Festival Lead” does not work for attacks it can use but does not actually have.   <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Lana’s Aid<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Put up to 3 in any combination of Pokémon that don't have a Rule Box and Basic Energy cards from your discard pile into your hand. <em>(Pokémon ex, Pokémon V, etc. have Rule Boxes.)</em></p>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div></div><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-2 span-2"><div class="sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image" data-block-type="5" data-sqsp-block="image-classic" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1708942353341_77616"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Love Ball<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Search your deck for a Pokémon with the same name as 1 of your opponent’s Pokémon in play, reveal it, and put it into your hand. Then, shuffle your deck.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Lucian<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Each player shuffles their hand and puts it on the bottom of their deck. If either player put any cards on the bottom of their deck in this way, each player flips a coin. If heads, that player draws 6 cards. If tails, they card 3 cards.</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Lucky Helmet<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If the Pokémon this card is attached to is in the Active Spot and is damaged by an attack from your opponent's Pokémon <em>(even if it is Knocked Out)</em>, draw 2 cards.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Reprint of </em><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/AOR/77" target="_blank"><em>Lucky Helmet AOR 77</em></a></p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Ogre’s Mask<br></strong><em>From Mask of Change</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Choose a Pokémon ex in your discard pile that has “Ogerpon” in its name, and switch it with 1 of your Pokémon ex in play that has “Ogrepon” in its name. </div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Perrin<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Reveal up to 2 Pokémon from your hand and put them into your deck.   <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Secret Box<br></strong><em>From Mask of Change</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">You can use this card only if you discard 3 other cards from your hand.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Search your deck for an Item card, a Pokémon Tool card, a Supporter card, and a Stadium card, reveal them, and put them into your hand. Then, shuffle your deck.</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Survival Brace<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If the Pokémon this card is attached to has full HP and would be Knocked Out by damage from an attack from your opponent’s Pokémon, that Pokémon is not Knocked Out, and its remaining HP becomes 10. Then, discard this card.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Unfair Stamp<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">You can play this card only if 1 of your Pokémon was Knocked Out during your opponent’s last turn.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Each player shuffles their hand into their deck. Then, you draw 5 cards, and your opponent draws 2 cards.</p>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="e2b4af0b00e059d33ba6" id="block-e2b4af0b00e059d33ba6"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Boomerang Energy<br></strong><em>From Crimson Haze</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">As long as this card is attached to a Pokémon, it provides [C<span style="text-decoration:underline">]</span>&nbsp;Energy.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If this card is discarded by the effect of an attack used by the Pokémon this card is attached to, attach this card from your discard pile to that Pokémon after attacking.</p>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div></div><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-2 span-2"><div class="sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image" data-block-type="5" data-sqsp-block="image-classic" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1712304012093_1660694"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Legacy Energy<br></strong><em>From Mask of Change</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">As long as this card is attached to a Pokémon, it provides every type of Energy but provides only 1 Energy at a time.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once per game, if the Pokémon this card is attached to is Knocked Out by damage from an attack from your opponent's Pokémon, your opponent takes 1 fewer Prize card. </p>
</div>
